CORRECTION TO THE AMENDED AND RESTATED HI-UTE RANCH
CONSERVATION EASEMENT
ThisCorrectiontotheAmended And RestatedHi-UteRanch ConservationEasement ismade
by and between Hi-Ute Ranch, L.C.,a Utah limitedliabilitycompany, havinga P.O.Box 17286,Salt
Lake City,Utah, 84117, ("Grantor")and The Utah Open Lands ConservationAssociation,Inc.,a
Utah non-profitcorporation("Grantee").
WHEREAS, Hi-Ute Holdings,L.C.,a Utah limitedliabilitycompany ("Hi-UteHoldings")
executeda ConservationEasement infavorof Grantee,datedDecember 28,2000 and recordedas
Entry00579403 inBook 01347 and Page 00556 intheSummit Country,Utah Recorder'soffice(the
"InitialEasement") encumbering a parcelofpropertylocatedinSummit County, Utah and as more
particularlydescribedinExhibit"A" attachedhereto(the"InitialParcel");
WHEREAS, Hi-Utah Holdings also granted a Conservation Easement (the "Second
Easement") in favorof Grantee encumbering propertyadjacentto the InitialParcel(the"Second
Property"),which Second Easement containedslightlydifferentterms and conditionsas the initial
Easement;
WHEREAS, Hi-UteHoldingsand Granteeexecutedan Amended and RestatedHi-UteRanch
ConservationEasement datedDecember I8,2007,thatwas recordedDecember 24,2007 asEntryNo.
00833384, inBook 1906,atPage 0541 (the"RestatedEasement"),which restatedand correctedthe
Second Easement;
WHEREAS, itwas theintentof Hi-Ute Holdingsand Granteetohave theInitialParcelbe
includedand subjecttothetermsoftheRestatedEasement,butasa resultofa scrivener'serror,the
legaldescriptionattachedtotheRestatedEasement onlyincludedthelegaldescriptionfortheSecond
Property;
WHEREAS, Grantorand Granteedesiretocorrectthiserror,ensuringtheintentoftheparties
atthetimewhich was toincludetheInitialParcelaspartoftheSecond Propertyencumbered by the
RestatedEasement;and
WHEREAS, Hi-UteHoldingswas merged intoGrantorpursuanttoArticlesofMerger dated
December 31,2008 and recordedwiththe Utah DivisionofCorporationsand Commercial Code on
January21,2009 and asaresultofthismerger,theInitialPropertyisnow owed bytheGrantor.
Now, therefore,inconsiderationoftheabove and tocorrectsucherror,Grantorand Grantee
herebyagreethattheInitialEasement encumberingtheInitialParcelisherebyamended and restatedin
itsentiretyand replacedwiththeRestatedEasement and thatsuch RestatedEasement iscorrectedso
thattheinitialParcelshallbe included,alongwiththeSecond Property,asthepropertyencumbered by
suchRestatedEasement.

applications. (6:04 P.M.)

Commissioner Hawley opened the meeting for public input at 6:04 p.m.

Commissioner Hawley closed the meeting for public input at 6:04 p.m.
357
2) Recognition of Bruce Carmichael for time on the Planning
Commission. (6:04 P.M.)
Attachment: Cover Page
Community Planning Director, Peter Barnes, read an email he had sent
to Bruce Carmichael thanking him for his service on the planning
commission. (6:05 P.M.)
Bruce Carmichael addressed the Commission. He thanked staff for their
work and thanked the new commission members for choosing to serve.
(6:08 P.M.)
3) Ratification of a motion to approve the Glenwild Phase II Amended
Subdivision Plat Amendment to adjust the subdivision boundary
line of the Common Area Parcel H. Applicant: Michael Kendell,
Summit County Project Engineer. Owner: Glenwild Community
Association. Administrative Review. Project #25-009. Tiffanie N.
Robinson, Senior Planner. (6:09 P.M.)
Attachment: Cover Page
Attachment: Staff Report Glenwild Phase 2
Senior Planner, Tiffanie Robinson, provided an overview of the agenda
item and outlined the reason for the need to ratify the motion that had
been made.
Matthew Nagie made a motion to ratify and clarify that the previous
motion was approval and ratify the approval of the Glenwild Phase
II Amended Subdivision Plat Amendment based on the findings of
fact, conclusions of law, and conditions of approval found in the
April 22, 2025, staff report. Spencer Young seconded, and all voted
in favor, (6-0).
4) Public hearing and action regarding a Conditional Use Permit
(CUP) for an "Accessory Dwelling Unit" (ADU) within Unit 5 of the
Altitude Loft Condominiums. 4123 N Forestdale Drive, Unit 5.
Parcel ALTLC-4. Applicant: Geoff Glade. Owner: The Crate of Ideas.

5.13.25

Attestation Letter
Planner Laura Kuhrmeyer presented a conditional use permit for an
accessory dwelling unit within Unit 5 of the Altitude Loft Condominiums.
Commissioner Peteroy asked a clarifying question regarding air
filtration. Justin Wallis, the project architect, stated that would be
clarified during the building code review.  (6:15 P.M.)
Commissioner Hawley opened the meeting for public hearing at 6:16
p.m.
No one came forward to speak.
Commissioner Hawley closed the meeting for public hearing at 6:16
p.m.

proposal to which Planner Kuhrmeyer and Director Barnes
responded. (6:17 P.M.)
Spencer Young made a motion to approve the applicant's request
for a Conditional Use Permit according to the findings of fact,
conclusions of law, and conditions of approval found in the staff
report. Matthew Nagie seconded, and all voted in favor, (6-0).

(CUP) for a Major Accessory Building (including an Accessory
Dwelling Unit) located on a Lot with an existing Single-Family

acres). Applicant & Owner: Julie E Ott. Administrative Process.

Attachment: Cover Page
Attachment: Project 25-038 Major Acc Bldg CUP Staff Report 5.13.25
Attachment: Exhibits A-E
Planner Kuhrmeyer introduced a proposal to add a major accessory
building in the Silver Creek area.
Commissioner Sagerman asked for clarification regarding the process of
reviewing the ADU that will be part of the proposal. Planner Kuhrmeyer
responded to his question. (6:25 P.M.)
James Snarr, a friend of the applicant, addressed how the non-ADU
portions of the building would be used by the owner. (6:26 P.M.)

p.m.

Julie Ott, Applicant, addressed the commission explaining her intentions
for the major accessory building. (6:35 P.M.)

Permit for a Major Accessory Building at 7596 Whileaway Road
West subject to the findings of fact, conclusions of law, and
conditions of approval found in the staff report. Heather Peteroy

(CUP) proposing the Uses of Light Manufacturing and Bar (Brewery
and Taproom) within an existing shell building. 4175 N Forestdale
Drive, Suite 105. Parcel PCBC-3 (0.84 acres). Applicant: Rob
Nielson. Owner: Topmark Investments. Administrative Process.

Attachment: Cover Page
Attachment: Project 25-051 Offset Brewery CUP SBPC 5.13.25
Attachment: Exhibits A-B
Planner Kuhrmeyer introduced a brewery and taproom conditional use
permit. She stated that staff has reviewed the application and
recommended approval.
Commissioner Sagerman asked about the hours of operation. Planner
Kuhrmeyer referred to the staff report for the proposed hours. He was
concerned about parking and asked for clarification about the expected
volume of patrons. (6:39 P.M.)
The owner of the brewery, Connor Brown, responded to the question
about potential traffic to the building. (6:40 P.M.)
Commissioner Young asked about feedback from the other business
owners in the area. Planner Kuhrmeyer provided information about the
types of other businesses that may have clients that would benefit from
the addition of the Brewery and Taproom. (6:42 P.M.)
Commissioner Peteroy asked for clarification about food offerings. Mr.
Brown stated there would not be any food preparation, but they plan to
offer snacks. (6:43 P.M.)
Rob Nielson, Owner, addressed the commission clarifying the number
of the parking spaces and the availability of stalls. (6:45 P.M.)
Commissioner Hawley opened the meeting for public hearing at 6:48
p.m.
No one came forward to speak.

Commissioner Sagerman suggested the creation of a formal process to inform
the other commissioners if they would not be attending a meeting.
Commissioner Sagerman asked for further clarification to help him understand
the process of allowing Accessory Dwelling Units. Discussion was held on the
topic.

Forestdale and how building codes get approved. Director Barnes responded to
her question and the discussion continued.
Lynda Viti, Civil Deputy Attorney, reminded Commissioners to complete the
Open and Public Meetings Act training and to let Commission Chair Mooney
know when it had been completed.
